HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000281/B002","root_000282/B002","root_001510/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_11b8ef3cba22431b3157","text":"3. **A hydraulic relief mechanism lies beneath the victory vocabulary.** `نَصْرُ` activates `ن ص ر B004` (relieving rain) and `ن ص ر B007` (tributary channels arriving from afar), while `ٱلْفَتْحُ` activates `ف ت ح B005` (water released through an opening). The water-collecting variant of `ج ي ء B002`, together with the later wide-passage image of `ف و ج B002`, makes the roots cohere materially: remote channels bring relief, an outlet opens, and flow spreads through a widened space. The actual ayat supply only arrival, aid, opening, entry, and groups; the water-flow direction is an analogical inference from their accepted branches. On this reading, victory is life-giving release and distribution, not only martial displacement.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001124/B001","root_001124/B003","root_001510/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_1ea2d8ab449d20ef5ab4","text":"`110:2` supplied the decisive test for the aperture reading: `يَدْخُلُونَ ... أَفْوَاجًا` turns `ف ت ح B001` into usable passage and activates repeated-coming within the variant inventory of `ج ي ء B001`. Its wide-space branch `ف و ج B002` also made the hydraulic reading unexpectedly coherent. `110:3` then corrected every endpoint reading of victory: praise recasts `ن ص ر B005` as gift, while forgiveness and return place the redress/judgment pairing (`ن ص ر B002`; `ف ت ح B003`) under continuing review.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_399a16729cf9c1b209f3","text":"110:2 made the access reading stronger than a simple victory reading: the proof of `فتح` is not possession but people actually entering. 110:3 then corrected the triumphal branch by requiring tasbīḥ and istighfār, which made judicial redress and mercy more salient than domination. The water-relief reading also gained force retrospectively from the repeated pattern of arrival, opening, entry, groups, covering, and return.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000281/B002","root_000282/B002","root_001124/B005","root_001510/B004","root_001510/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_4c20d6eb242cb99ed491","text":"2. **Victory as flowing relief, not only domination.** Lexical evidence: `ن ص ر` B004, rain and relief by rain; `ن ص ر` B007, tributary watercourse; `ف ت ح` B005, water released from its opening; `ج ي ء` B002/B003, water-collecting hollow; `س م و` B004, sky/rain; `ر ب ب` B008/B013, cloud and abundant water; `ف و ج` B001, successive groups. Structural cue: the sequence runs from coming help/opening to people entering in waves, and the branch inventories cluster around rain, water release, tributaries, and collected flow. Assigned role: `نصر` becomes relief-rain, `فتح` becomes the opened outlet, `أفواج` becomes the visible wave pattern downstream. Abductive move: I supply a hydrological causal path: sky/rain relief opens channels, and groups move like tributaries into a collection point. Reading change: \"نصر الله والفتح\" is activated as life-giving release and irrigation, not merely as military conquest.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001510/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:5","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_726e31795a2f1f584fd2","text":"5. **What comes is a gift that creates praise rather than human credit.** `نَصْرُ` anchors `ن ص ر B005` (granting or gift), reinforced by the genitive `ٱللَّهِ`; `ح م د B001` later names thanksgiving for benefaction, whereas `ح م د B005` exposes the counter-possibility of claiming praise because of a favor. The packet supplies divine gift followed by praise; I infer that the final command regulates ownership of credit. `نَصْرُ ٱللَّهِ` therefore reads as a received good whose arrival creates an obligation of thanks and blocks the beneficiary from converting success into personal merit.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000281/B001","root_000282/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_8728eeb505c1f3c64e3f","text":"2. **The human groups are the visible form of a repeated coming.** The second accepted variant within `ج ي ء B001` is prevailing by repeated coming, and `ف و ج B001` in `أَفْوَاجًا` supplies successive human batches. The singular event `جَآءَ` is immediately unpacked as many arrivals through the imperfect plural `يَدْخُلُونَ`. The packet gives singular arrival followed by successive groups; I infer that the latter manifests the former rather than merely following it. Thus the coming of aid is read distributively: its prevalence becomes visible through wave after wave of entrants.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001124/B003","root_001510/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_894167eb5c392c22ba36","text":"3. **Opening as adjudication and redress.** Lexical evidence: `ن ص ر` B002, redress after oppression; `ف ت ح` B003, settling a closed dispute by judgment; `د ي ن` B002, reckoning/recompense; `غ ف ر` B002, shielding from consequence; `ت و ب` B003, calling someone to repent/return. Structural cue: 110:1 pairs help with opening; 110:2 locates the mass movement in dīn; 110:3 answers with forgiveness and return. Assigned role: help/redress handles the oppressed side; opening/judgment resolves the closed case; dīn/reckoning supplies the juridical domain; istighfār/tawwāb supplies post-judgment mercy. Abductive move: I infer that an opened victory can be a court-like settlement whose end is not punishment alone but return. Reading change: the ayah reads as a decisive divine ruling that opens the way to pardon, not only as the winning of a contest.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001510/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_982bdb9ea3b6c0b3e040","text":"4. **Opening is also a verdict that places victory under moral review.** `ن ص ر B002` contributes redress after oppression and `ف ت ح B003` the settling of a closed dispute by judgment. Later, `د ي ن B002` supplies reckoning/recompense, and `ٱسْتَغْفِرْهُ` plus the final repentance clause supplies post-outcome accountability. Redress and judgment assign the event a juridical role; I infer from the consequent command that even a favorable verdict does not exempt its beneficiary from review. The ayah consequently reads as adjudicated redress whose proper sequel is self-audit, not unrestricted triumph.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000281/B001","root_000281/B004","root_000282/B001","root_001124/B001","root_001124/B006","root_001510/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_a20c93ec7c47437ac772","text":"1. **Help and opening as an arrival-threshold that creates access.** Lexical evidence: `ج ي ء` B001, coming/arrival; `ج ي ء` B004, bringing or presenting something; `ن ص ر` B001, aid that makes someone prevail; `ف ت ح` B001, opening what is closed; `ف ت ح` B006, key/means of access; `د خ ل` B001, entering. Structural cue: 110:1 says the help/opening \"comes\"; 110:2 immediately shows people entering; 110:3 gives the required response once the threshold has opened. Assigned role: coming marks event-arrival, help supplies efficacy, opening supplies aperture, and entry verifies that the aperture works. Abductive move: I infer that the later entry is the functional test of the earlier opening. Reading change: the ayah reads less as an abstract announcement of victory and more as the moment when a closed boundary becomes passable.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001510/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_c0afde0c906f732bdc6d","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"110:1:3\",\"root_001510\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Yardım, insanın kendi başarısı sayacağı bir kazançtan çok, övgüyü verene yöneltmesi gereken bir armağan olarak okunur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000281/B001","root_000282/B001","root_001124/B001","root_001510/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_cc3fba64ed4589776f2f","text":"1. **Arrival opens a route rather than merely announcing an endpoint.** `جَآءَ` anchors `ج ي ء B001`, specifically its coming/arrival variant; `نَصْرُ` anchors `ن ص ر B001` (aid that makes another prevail); and `ٱلْفَتْحُ` anchors `ف ت ح B001` (opening a closed or widened aperture). The conditional `إِذَا` makes this arrival the trigger, while `يَدْخُلُونَ` in `110:2` supplies the motion that an aperture enables. Aid is the enabling force and opening is the traversable result; the directional claim that aid produces access is inferred from their sequence and the later entry. The ayah therefore reads less as two synonymous victory terms and more as a causal mechanism: enabling support arrives, a closure gives way, and passage becomes possible.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000047/B002","root_001124/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_ee7c8eeda9fc8b3230ab","text":"4. **The beginning of a final liturgical sequence.** Lexical evidence: `ف ت ح` B002, opening beginning; `ء ل ه` B002, invocation formula; `س ب ح` B001, worship/prayer; `ح م د` B001, praise/thanksgiving. Structural cue: the packet itself begins with basmalah, 110:1 begins the ordinary surah movement with \"when,\" and 110:3 supplies imperatives of worship. Assigned role: `فتح` opens not only territory/access but a ritual sequence: invocation, event, witnessed entry, praise, and forgiveness. Abductive move: I infer that the temporal `إذا` functions as a hinge: when this beginning arrives, a particular worship-response becomes due. Reading change: 110:1 becomes an opening cue for liturgical action, not only a reportable historical condition.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001124/B003","root_001510/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_f471c0fbbe257dfc8e28","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"110:1:3\",\"root_001510\",[\"B002\"]],[\"110:1:5\",\"root_001124\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Bu zafer, ezilenin hakkını teslim eden ve kapanmış bir uyuşmazlığı hükme bağlayan Allah'ın kararı boyutu taşır; sonraki bağışlanma buyruğu, kazananı da ahlaki denetimin dışında bırakmaz.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001124/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_fdc876b369f174c0f72f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"110:1:5\",\"root_001124\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Zaferdeki başlangıç boyutu, bu anı ardından övgü ve bağışlanma gerektiren ibadet dizisinin açılış işareti hâline getirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
